────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  10 · ALL ACCURACY / PRO-PROCEDURE TIPS
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  01. Primary product = gold_standard named definition (GS-MAP etc.), not a NASA GRS answer.
  02. Absolute System III needs good CM: paste WinJUPOS CM or use SPICE — analytic CM is weaker.
  03. Paste your WinJUPOS *manual* lon/lat to validate automation (WJ does not auto-detect GRS).
  04. Keep one fixed definition across nights for drift science.
  05. Report definition id + CM source + σ always — that is publication hygiene.
  06. Process full always runs AUTO limb (green) + BY EYE limb (cyan) — dual measure.
  07. JUPOS: auto outline is approximate; fine-tune cyan outline on the true limb by eye.
  08. Keys: arrows move outline, PgUp/PgDn size, R = reset cyan to green, drag = centre.
  09. Publish lon: GS-MAP dark core. Edges for size only — core ≠ outline.
  10. Same definition (core vs core) when comparing to WinJUPOS or prior nights.
  11. Mid-exposure UTC only; ~0.6° System III per minute of time error (BAA).
  12. Trusted CM: SPICE / Horizons / WinJUPOS CML — not analytical for absolute publish.
  13. NASA Horizons = planet geometry only, NOT a GRS longitude catalog.
  14. Prefer red channel for GRS; check E–W flip if lon is wildly wrong.
  15. Paste WinJUPOS lon/lat → equality Δsky ″ is the real accuracy test.
  16. dual_measure.json: auto vs human Δsky — large Δ = definition/limb sensitivity.
  17. Method soup / SOTA = scatter only; never report as the official centre.
  18. MC iterations: 50 quick, 200 good, 500–1000 research-grade (slower).
  19. Injection trials: 16 draft, 28–36 solid, 48–64 tight bias calibration.
  20. σ_tot ≤ 1–2″ is an honest ground-based target for an extended cloud feature.
  21. Lat ~−22° typical; large lat offsets often mean limb/nav issues.
  22. Truth recovery arcsec only on synthetics (known ground truth).
  23. Multi-epoch drift needs ≥2 nights with the same definition.
  24. Process full = AUTO limb (green) + BY EYE limb (cyan) in one job.
  25. JUPOS: automatic outline is only a first guess — always fine-tune by eye.
  26. WinJUPOS: arrows move outline; Page Up/Down change size; fit the true limb, not the belts.
  27. Put cyan on the planet edge; if cyan is too small/large, lon and lat both shift.
  28. Recommended: GS-MAP+RIM = core lon/lat (GS-MAP) + outer W–E rim for length.
  29. Publish longitude: dark core (GS-MAP). Outer rim = size only — not the published lon.
  30. Use edges (OUTLINE / W–E) for size/extent — not as a silent substitute for core lon.
  31. Same definition every night (core vs core) when comparing to WinJUPOS or past runs.
  32. Mid-exposure UTC of the stack only — not start time, not wall-clock now.
  33. BAA: ~0.6° System III per minute of timing error — clocks matter.
  34. Stacks should span only a few minutes for cartography (JUPOS image requirements).
  35. Trusted CM: SPICE / Horizons / WinJUPOS CML / override — not bare analytical for publish.
  36. NASA Horizons = geometry (CM, distance), NOT a GRS longitude catalog.
  37. Prefer red / visual-red channel for GRS contrast; blue is often weaker for this feature.
  38. Check orientation: N-up, E–W not mirrored (use flip checkboxes if stack is reversed).
  39. Paste your WinJUPOS lon/lat to test equality (Δsky ″) — that is the real accuracy check.
  40. dual_measure.json: large Δsky usually means definition or limb, not “NASA wrong.”
  41. Method soup / SOTA = scatter only — never the published centre.
  42. Quality flags: lat outside GRS band or untrusted CM → do not treat as absolute System III.
  43. On synthetics, truth_recovery sky_error_arcsec is the honest self-test metric.
